- **Prior session (2026-07-06, P8 — landing cosmos GLOW fidelity (owner's 6th "still has differences — the design's planets/colours/glow are better"): FIXED — code green + verified by looking.** Root cause found by reading the design source (`Sanad Landing.dc.html`) end-to-end + web-confirming the three **r152** colour-pipeline change: the `landingWorlds` colours/sizes/positions and the whole scene port **already matched the design EXACTLY** — the ONLY gap was the **bloom**. The design runs three **r128**; we run **r171**, whose `UnrealBloomPass` switched its internal render targets to **`HalfFloatType` (HDR)** (r128 used 8-bit `RGBAFormat`, which clamps) → at the reference `0.85/0.7/0.2` the r171 pass blooms the additive glow layers **far wider** into a grey haze, so the prior fidelity pass (2026-07-04) fought it by dialing every glow input DOWN (threshold 0.2→**0.45**, planet halo `rad·4.4`→3.4 / `0.05+0.5`→`0.04+0.3`, world-veil `0.22+0.55`→`0.08+0.26`) — which stopped the planet **bodies** from blooming and made them read flat/dim (the exact complaint). **Fix:** vendored the **exact r128** `UnrealBloomPass` (+`Pass`/`CopyShader`/`LuminosityHighPassShader`) verbatim into `apps/website/src/motion/cosmos/vendor/postprocessing/` (8-bit `RGBAFormat` = bounded/tight bloom; lint-ignored as upstream source; `Pass` adds an inert `isPass=true` for r171 `EffectComposer.addPass` type-compat), pointed `bloom.tsx` at it, and **restored all reference numbers** (`BLOOM.threshold` **0.2**, halo **4.4** / `0.05+0.5·pres`, veil **0.22+0.55·peak** with the design's `62% 54% at 50% 50%` / α0.14 gradient). Codified **WEB11 as a body rule** (was change-log-only) — match a hand-tuned older-three scene by its **pass version + pipeline**, NEVER by re-tuning a different-version pass. **Gates:** `nx lint/test/build website` green (**185 tests**; `next build` typecheck OK — vendored `.js` is `allowJs`; added `allowJs` to `tsconfig.spec.json` too) + **verifier PASS**. **Verified by looking (U21e):** restarted `sanad-dev-website-1` (fresh compile), headless **system-Chrome** shot of `:8190/ar` — the hero star now renders as a **bright glowing cyan orb** (was a dim moon), matching design image 4; `scrollWidth==innerWidth` on `/ar`+`/en` (no RTL overflow). **Scope (owner decision):** planets/glow only — the live hero headline + logo differ via **CMS/settings content** (the coded defaults in `landing-copy.ts` are already the design wording), left untouched. boot-verifier not re-run (frontend-only, stack already UP; website container recompiled clean → `GET /ar 200`).
  - **Follow-up same day (owner looked: "grey layer still there + sections don't appear at the planet"):** two more cosmos fixes — the r128 pass alone wasn't enough. **(1)** r171's `EffectComposer` defaults its buffers to `HalfFloatType` (HDR) so the bloom still hazed wide → construct it with an explicit **8-bit `UnsignedByteType`/`RGBAFormat`** render target (`bloom.tsx`), clamping the input like r128 → deep space near-black. **(2)** **squared** the `world-veil` `presencePeak` triangle so the reference `0.22+0.55` wash is tight to the dock, not a wide ramp that greyed space between worlds. **The "sections don't appear" was NOT a design bug — I had crashed the stack:** `docker restart sanad-dev-website-1` (to pick up a frontend edit) re-ran its `nx run-many build` CMD, rewrote shared `dist/`, and crash-looped the **api-gateway** (`ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND: @sanad/util/dist`, 502) → `getPortfolio()` failed → `TrustSection`/`WorksSection` render `null` (need categorised projects). Recovered per OPS5 (`touch apps/api-gateway/dist/main.js`, no rebuild → gateway 200 in ~5s; portfolio returns 5 categorised projects). **Lesson (added to `dev-stack-ops` + rules WEB11/OPS5): a frontend SOURCE edit needs NO container restart — `next dev` HMR recompiles it (`✓ Compiled` in the container logs); restarting is only for a new dep and cascades the backend.** Re-verified by looking: trust chapter shows heading + eyebrow + lead + all 5 domain chips arced from a bright teal planet, background near-black, content opacity 1 at the dock. Typecheck (`tsc -p apps/website/tsconfig.json` exit 0) + eslint on the changed files clean; `next build` NOT run on host (would clobber the live `next dev` container's `.next`).

Lowered to the reference **0.2** (`intensity 0.9`, `smoothing 0.08`, `radius 0.7`): the earlier haze worry is already handled by the **sRGB-tagged fog/clear** keeping deep space near-black (~0 luminance stays below threshold), so ONLY the bright planets/star/coin/limbs bloom, not the space between. **Verified by looking:** hero star, trust gas-giant + services Saturn ring now read as **luminous glowing orbs** matching the design, while deep space stays near-black (no grey haze). `tsc` clean; finale spec green (bloom base 0.9 flows into the finale surge).
  - **Follow-up #2 (2026-07-04, owner: "nothing happened, planets still dark" after the threshold change).** Two root causes. **(a) The bloom ALGORITHM, not just the threshold.** Rendering the reference `.dc.html` in the same headless Chrome proved headless is unreliable for the cosmos — the reference's WebGL planets are **invisible in headless SwiftShader** (its EffectComposer/UnrealBloomPass doesn't composite there), while our R3F scene renders. So the real gap was that `@react-three/postprocessing`'s `Bloom` is a **different, weaker** algorithm than the reference's `UnrealBloomPass` and never matched even at equal params. **Fixed by porting three's OWN `UnrealBloomPass`** (`bloom.tsx` now builds a manual `EffectComposer`: `RenderPass → UnrealBloomPass(res, 0.85, 0.7, 0.2) → OutputPass`, driven at `useFrame` priority 1; `BLOOM = {strength,radius,threshold}`; `BloomHandle.strength`; finale surges `.strength`). Removed the `@react-three/postprocessing` dep (+ lockfile-only). By-looking (headless, which under-shows the reference entirely): hero/trust/services planets now bloom into **luminous glowing orbs** (soft wide glow, bright edges/rings) — the design look; no console/page errors (composer runs clean). **(b) Browser cache** — the owner "saw nothing" partly because Next dev + browser cache served the old chunks; **restarted the website container** for a clean rebuild (fresh chunk hashes) — owner must **hard-refresh** (Cmd+Shift+R). This uses the EXACT reference pass + params + lighting, so it will match the design on the owner's real GPU (headless SwiftShader over-blows/hazes bloom, so headless is only a "does it render + glow" check, not a brightness gauge — confirm final intensity on the real GPU).

  - **Stars looked washed/desaturated (root: R3F default ACES tone-map + soft bloom).** Added **`flat`** to `<Canvas>` (`cosmos-canvas.tsx`) → `NoToneMapping` (the reference renders on raw-Three's flat pipeline). Also reduced the `world-veil` wash + the star corona/halo/atmosphere additive opacities & sizes (`world-planets.tsx`) and the coin halo (0.55→0.32).
  - **"Sections don't appear with the planet when the meteor reaches it" (root: content vs planet were desynced).** `presence.ts` docks the planet across a plateau **[0.34→0.84]**, but `content-flow.ts flowTransform` was settling content only at the single midpoint 0.5. **Re-derived `flowTransform` to a planet-synced reveal plateau** (arrive ~0.32 → hold across the docked window → recede ~0.82) so the section is composed _with_ its world the whole dock. Spec `cosmos-motion.spec.ts` updated first (TDD).

Local `tsc -p apps/website --noEmit` clean; cosmos math/motion specs **41 green**; chapter-stage + landing-sections specs green. **`verifier` agent PASS** (run via direct `npx tsc/eslint/vitest` + real `next build` since the stack was UP, OPS5-sanctioned — typecheck+lint+build green, **158 website tests**, rule-greps clean; U6/U18 WebGL carve-outs only). `website.md` updated (DOC1).

Restarting the container (`docker restart sanad-dev-website-1`) re-runs its CMD (`nx build contracts,config,web-ui`), which rewrote shared dist and tripped the known **mid-rebuild `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND`** on content-service (container up, restarts=0, app subprocess crashed → gateway content calls 500). Recovered per the playbook: `touch apps/<svc>/dist/main.js` across the 6 backend services → content/portfolio + branding + health all 200; `/ar` renders 200/158KB with the 5 seeded case studies + all fidelity fixes intact. **Lesson:** to clear a wedged Next cache, restart the website container directly (don't `rm .next` live) and be ready for the shared-dist touch-recovery. **Remaining (follow-up, documented — not silently skipped):** per-section content fine-polish (typography/card treatments) beyond the systemic cosmos+nav fixes; the reference's bottom-right telemetry HUD is not built. **Config note:** set `.claude/settings.local.json` `defaultMode` → `bypassPermissions` (owner asked to stop permission prompts).

Bloom to the reference **0.85 / 0.7 / 0.2** and exposed via a shared ref for the finale surge.

- **This session (2026-07-01, P8 Stage 8.B-3 — the sound layer (WEB8): DONE — verifier PASS + boot-verifier UP + verified by looking):** Closed the last deferred piece of the landing canon — Sanad's audible signature. **website now 146 tests** (+20). **2 owner decisions (plan-mode):** (1) **audio source = procedural Web Audio** (synthesized oscillators + gain envelopes; **no sample files, no npm deps** — the public media resolver is still deferred to 8.C, and this keeps the layer asset-free, tokened, pure-core testable); (2) **cue set = the full signature layer** (ambient bed + section-arrival chime + primary-CTA tone).
  - **Pure cores (`src/motion/audio/`, the TDD heart — no DOM/AudioContext, unit-tested like `cosmos/math`).** `cue-defs.ts` (one source of the musical/mix constants — a **minor-pentatonic** run, one note per world from `@sanad/config` `landingWorldOrder`; low master ceiling + quiet cue gains) + `audio-graph.ts` (`sectionIndexAt` = nearest-world/arrival event; `chimeFrequency` = per-world pentatonic note; `ctaFrequency` = fifth above root; `ambientGain(warp)` = bed loudness rising with scroll velocity, hard-capped low + monotonic; `envelopeGain` = cue attack/decay). N3 honored — time is the audio clock, never `Date.now()`.
  - **Client shell.** `audio-engine.ts` — a thin wrapper over ONE `AudioContext`, created **lazily on `unlock()`** (a real gesture → no autoplay); quiet master bus, two-oscillator ambient bed, one-shot chime/CTA tones, all scheduled off `ctx.currentTime`; `ctxFactory` is injectable so a mock context drives the spec (jsdom has no real `AudioContext`) — asserts **no context before `unlock()`** + cues silent while disabled. `use-sound-preference.ts` — persisted on/off, **default OFF**, SSR-safe (first render false → effect hydrates `localStorage['sanad-sound']`, no hydration mismatch). `audio-context.ts` + `audio-provider.tsx` (mounted **inside** `MotionProvider` so it reads the same `CosmosScrollStore` the cosmos does): per-frame maps `warp`→bed loudness + fires a chime when the nearest section changes; a **delegated** `click` on `.lp-cta--primary` plays the CTA tone (so the **server-rendered** CTA links stay untouched, WEB2); a persisted-ON preference still waits for the first gesture (no-autoplay); under `prefers-reduced-motion` `available=false` → engine never starts ("calm mode" = motion + sound off, WEB4).
  - **Toggle + chrome.** `components/sound-toggle.tsx` — the persistent, always-visible control in the top-nav actions beside the locale switch (`aria-pressed`, `dict.audio` labels, inline speaker/mute SVG via `currentColor`); toggling ON is itself the unlocking gesture; under calm mode it renders visible-but-disabled. Wired into `top-nav.tsx` (client component inside the server nav) + `app/[locale]/layout.tsx` (`<AudioProvider>` inside `<MotionProvider>`). i18n `audio:{on,off,label}` in `ar.ts`/`en.ts` (parity-enforced). `.sound-toggle` styles in `site-chrome.css` reuse existing `--web-*` tokens only (**no `@sanad/config` change** → config/backend dist untouched → no OPS5 cascade). No new npm deps, no binary assets, no gateway routes (**no Postman drift, DOC2**).

- **This session (2026-07-01, P8 Stage 8.B-2 — R3F WebGL cosmos + sticky-chapter journey: BUILT, closing gates):** Closed the 8.B-1 design gap to reach parity with `Sanad Landing.dc.html` (U21 — verified **by looking** at `/ar` + `/en` with a Playwright/system-Chrome screenshot harness, not just green tests). **website now 126 tests** (+60). **2 owner decisions (plan-mode):** (1) **scope = (a)–(e) this phase, defer the sound layer (f/WEB8) to 8.B-3** — the visual cosmos + chapters + veil + moons + finale share one scroll engine and are coherent; sound is separable. (2) **WebGL approach = port the exact `cosmos.js` Three.js math verbatim, hosted in an R3F `<Canvas>`** (R3F _is_ Three.js under a React renderer → same library, identical behaviour, satisfies the WEB3 R3F stack). **8 TDD sub-stages A→H.**
  - **Scroll engine (`src/motion/scroll/`).** A tiny **ref-backed `CosmosScrollStore`** (NOT React state → no per-frame re-render), created once in `MotionProvider` and fed from the Lenis scroll event + `pointermove` cursor; provided via `CosmosScrollContext`. The R3F `useFrame` reads a live snapshot each frame + calls `tick()` (the `stepWarp` velocity→warp exponential smoothing); DOM overlays subscribe. Under reduced-motion progress still updates (static fallback) but velocity/warp/cursor stay 0. `section-progress.localProgress` for sticky chapters. All unit-tested.
  - **R3F cosmos (`src/motion/cosmos/`, lazy `next/dynamic` `ssr:false`).** `CosmosMount` (replaces the deleted DOM `CentralAnchor`) gates the canvas on desktop-motion-fine-pointer, else a **static token glow** (WEB4/WEB10 mobile). `CosmosCanvas` re-provides the store context _inside_ the Canvas (R3F is a separate reconciler root). `CosmosScene` = one imperative `useFrame` driving the camera flight (`cameraY`=progress×2000 + cursor parallax); `SanadCoin` (halo sprite + 2 tori + wireframe/solid octahedron gem, `coinTransform`), `WorldPlanets` (9 planets arrive→dock→exit via `planetPresence`, ringed services/works/cta, per-world palettes), `Starfield` (3 soft round parallax layers following the camera), `WarpStreaks` (velocity-gated hyperspace), `SceneLighting` (fog + sun **lerp between world glows**), `CosmosBloom` (`@react-three/postprocessing` UnrealBloom) each own a `useFrame` reading the store.
  - **Pure math (`cosmos/math/`, the TDD heart — every scalar unit-tested against the reference constants to lock fidelity).** `smoothstep`/`lerp`/`clamp`, `cameraY`/parallax/lookAt, `planetPresence`, `coinTransform`, `worldColorAt`, `warpStreak`/`warpStreakCount`, `layerRotation`, `moonPosition`/`moonRingVisibility`, `flowTransform`, `gridFan`, `footerReveal`, `emberDelay`/`emberTransform`, `footItemReveal`, + `worksOrbitTransform` **extending** `orbit.ts` (old `orbitTransform` + its tests kept intact). N3 honored — time is the rAF `clock`, never `Date.now()`.
  - **Tokens (U6, one OPS5 config change).** Added **`landingWorlds`** (9 worlds `{glow, palette[3], type, radius, ring}`) + `landingWorldOrder` to `@sanad/config` `tokens.ts` (+ `tokens.spec` 9-count/hex assertion); `cosmos/worlds/world-defs.ts` derives the runtime scene fields (3D offsets — U6 carve-out) + hex→`ColorTriplet` once at load, so WebGL reads typed constants while the DOM veil/footer read the same `landingWorlds` glow (one colour source). Fog init reads `websiteTokens.surface.bg`; the coin's white wireframe is a named `COIN.gemWire` constant.
  - **Sticky chapters (`components/landing/chapter/`).** Each section (except hero) wrapped in a tall **180–320vh `ChapterStage`** whose sticky stage pins while its content **arrives from depth → settles at the midpoint → recedes** (`flowTransform` applied to an _inner_ wrapper via one GSAP ScrollTrigger, so the sticky element itself is never transformed — sticky stays intact). Server children pass straight through (WEB2). Normal one-screen flow under reduced-motion/SSR (existing `Reveal` staggers). `CHAPTER_HEIGHTS` map (U18). **Works kept its own pin** (evolves to the spine-orbit, below), not double-wrapped.
  - **Overlays.** `cosmos/world-veil.tsx` (fixed `mix-blend:screen` wash, `worldColorAt` + `presencePeak`, 0.22–0.77 opacity); `landing/process/process-moons.tsx` (4 phase-moons orbiting the process planet, active moon rides to top + glows, own ScrollTrigger, RTL `dir`); evolved `works-orbit.tsx` → `worksOrbitTransform` (cards circle the **cosmos coin spine**, front sharp/clickable + back recede/blur; dropped the DOM axis line + pushed the works planet back to z-240 so cards read); `landing/finale/` = **impact re-entry** (300vh, fading label) + **fixed footer finale** (`footerReveal`-driven; crater glow + `craterPulse`, 12 rising embers `emberRise` staggered by `emberDelay`, footer content cascading via `footItemReveal`; brand/headline/tagline/CTA/nav/legal, coded bilingual copy). RTL via `dir`/logical props; all settle to static under reduced-motion (`@media` disables embers/pulse).
  - **Deps (React-19-compatible set, one container dep-refresh).** `three@0.171` + `@react-three/fiber@9.6` + `@react-three/drei@10.7` + `@react-three/postprocessing@3.0` + `@types/three`. Fixed a **pre-existing 8.B-1 RTL bug** found while verifying (`.lp-cta-glow` used `inset-inline-start:50%`+`translate(-50%)` — not direction-aware — causing horizontal overflow / an RTL left-gutter; switched to direction-agnostic auto-margin centering, WEB6).
  - **Gates GREEN.** **Verifier PASS** (stack DOWN → `nx run-many -t typecheck lint test build --projects=website,config,contracts,web-ui` all green — **`next build` succeeds with the R3F cosmos**; 126 website tests; rule-greps clean: no `any`/`new Date()`/`Date.now()`/raw-hex-at-call-site/physical-left-right; ar/en parity). One verifier catch fixed: `@react-three/drei` was declared but never imported (I built the scene with raw R3F + `@react-three/postprocessing`, no drei) → removed it from `package.json` (`@nx/dependency-checks`). **Boot-verifier UP** — 12/12 containers restarts=0, gateway `/api/health` 200, `/`→308→`/ar`=`<html lang=ar dir=rtl>` with the cosmos-layer + `lp-hero`/`lp-chapter`/`lp-impact`/`lp-finale` + all 5 seeded case studies (مسار/وصلة/رصيد/إتقان/رعاية), `/en` ltr, `GET /api/content/portfolio`→200 (5), `/admin/`→200; **the config rebuild + website dep-refresh did NOT cascade the backend** (additive config change). **Verified by looking (U21e):** Playwright + system Chrome screenshots of `/ar` (hero cosmos, trust/services/process settled chapters, works spine-orbit, process moons, footer finale) + `/en` (LTR). **Docs:** `website.md` cosmos section (DOC1); **no gateway routes → no Postman drift (DOC2).**

- **This session (2026-06-29, ROOT-CAUSE FIX — the chronic "Upstream service error"):** Owner reported the recurring "works a little then errors, sometimes per-service (settings still loads)" failure. Diagnosed live to the true cause: the **request/reply command consumers were `noAck:false`, but NestJS never auto-acks a `@MessagePattern`** — only the event consumers manual-acked. Every command was handled + replied (gateway 200) but never acknowledged → each held a prefetch slot → after `prefetchCount` 10 the consumer went silent (content first, settings later). **Fix:** `createRmqServerOptions` gained a `noAck` flag; every service's **commands** `connectMicroservice` now passes `noAck:true` (reply = the ack for RPC; auto-ack avoids redelivering half-done writes); **events keep `noAck:false` + manual ack** (MS6). Rebuilt messaging dist + nudged all 5 services. **Verified: 25 back-to-back content requests = 25×200, all `*.commands` queues at 0 unacked** (was climbing +1/request forever). Rule added (MS6 clarified, 2026-06-29); `dev-stack-ops` "burst-of-10 stall" note corrected (it was this bug). 5 messaging tests + content-service typecheck green.

  - **Toast** (`src/data/toast/*` + `src/components/feedback/toast/*`): a module-level singleton store (mirrors `EventBus`, read via `useSyncExternalStore` — **not** Redux, since toasts carry non-serializable action callbacks). ## Boot proof (2026-07-09, P8 Stage 8.D — one universe + comet-flight navigation)

Stack cycled DOWN (for the code verifier, OPS5) → UP. **12/12 containers up and stable, judged by logs** (the cycle surfaced + recovered: the cold-boot `gateway_nx` recursive-task wedge ×2 and `media_nx` ×1 — rm container + drop `*_nx` volume + up -d; and the NEW OPS5 gotcha, sibling watchers dying permanently on `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND` during a single-container restart's shared-dist rewipe — see the 2026-07-09 OPS5 change-log entry). Infra healthy (postgres/rabbitmq/redis). Through nginx `:8190`: `GET /api/health` → 200; `GET /api/content/services?pageSize=1` → 200 envelope (items=1, total=4 — gateway→RMQ→content proven); `/admin/` → 200. **Website SSR:** `/` → 308 → `/ar` (`lang="ar" dir="rtl"`) with `data-system="landing"` + `cosmos-layer` + `page-stage data-travel-phase="idle"` + the AccentStyle `:has()` rules; `/en/services` ltr `data-system="services"`; `/ar/services/erp` `data-system="serviceDetail" data-system-slug="erp"`; `/ar/contact` `data-system="contact"`; shell carries NO inline `--web-accent` (route mode). **Verified by looking on the cycled stack (U21e):** the full flight matrix re-ran CLEAN (calm-mode veil flight, 390px fallback no-overflow, 5-hop journey each landing `idle` overflow 0, Escape-skip), and the 404 page in a real browser mounts `data-system="ambient"` + the ambient accent + the not-found state over the live canvas.

## Boot proof (2026-06-30, P7.D — dashboard + user-management + leads/notifications)

Full stack up — **11/11 containers, RestartCount=0** (6 backend apps + admin-portal Vite + nginx + postgres/rabbitmq/redis healthy). Through nginx (`:8190`): `GET /api/health` → 200; `GET /admin/` → 200 `<html lang="ar" dir="rtl" data-app data-theme="dark">`. Admin login (`admin@sanad.tech` / coded default `change-me-admin`) → token. **New routes mapped + working:** `GET /api/dashboard/stats` → 200 **real fan-out payload** (`counts {leadsTotal:29, leadsNew:29, users:1, services:4, blogPosts:1, unreadNotifications:24}`, `recentLeads:5`, `recentActivity:5` — proves the gateway aggregation across content/identity/notifications); `GET /api/identity/roles` → 200 (7 `RoleSummary`); `GET /api/leads` + `/api/notifications` → 200. **User write path proven at runtime (N14 tx + RBAC):** `POST /api/identity/users` → 201 (roles=[developer]) → search shows it → `DELETE …/roles/developer` → 200 (roles=[]) → `PATCH …{isActive:false}` → 200. **No-token guards:** `GET /api/dashboard/stats` + `POST /api/identity/users` → 401. **The 4 new admin Vite modules transform → 200 `text/javascript`** (`notifications-bell`/`leads-page`/`dashboard-page`/`users-page` — **no dep-refresh needed**, zero new npm deps; the host `libs/contracts/dist` was current so the new exports resolved without an in-container `nx build contracts`).

**Boot incident (recovered, environment-only — no code change, refines OPS5/`dev-stack-ops`).** The cold `up -d --build` raced the **api-gateway** into the known `NX Recursive task invocation (api-gateway:serve -> api-gateway:serve)` wedge (the documented stubborn case — `/api/health` 502 internally while the other 5 services booted clean). Recovered per the playbook: `docker compose rm -sf api-gateway` → `docker volume rm sanad-dev_gateway_nx` → `up -d api-gateway` → `restart nginx` (re-resolve the new upstream IP). Then gateway listened, `/api/health` 200, Recursive count back to 0. **Lesson:** a `up -d --build` that recreates all backend app containers on cold `.nx` volumes still races the gateway — prefer staggered app-container startup (or `up -d` without `--build` once images exist) to avoid the one-time gateway recreate.

## Boot proof (2026-06-28, P7.C Stage 3 — blog + rich-text editor)

Full stack up — **11/11 containers, restarts=0** after a `*_nx`-volume recovery (below). Through nginx (`:8190`): `GET /api/health` → 200; `GET /admin/` → 200 `<html … dir="rtl">`. **New Vite modules + the new `quill` dep transform (the stale-node_modules 500 check) → 200 `text/javascript`:** `…/modules/blog/components/blog-page.tsx`, `…/components/rich-text/rich-text-editor.tsx`, and the **optimized `quill@2.0.3` chunk** (`node_modules/.vite/apps/admin-portal/deps/quill.js`, 474 KB) + its snow CSS — **the dep-refresh succeeded, no 500 on the quill import.** Backend the vertical consumes (admin token, `admin@sanad.tech` / coded default `change-me-admin` since `ADMIN_PASSWORD` unset in dev): `GET /api/content/blog/manage` → 200 (draft list), `/api/content/blog-categories` → 200, `/api/content/blog-tags` → 200, `/api/content/team` → 200. Gateway maps the full blog + blog-categories + blog-tags + team route set (pre-existing P5 B4 — **no new gateway routes, no Postman drift**). Verifier PASS across all 14 projects (the Vite build bundled `quill`).

**Dep-refresh recovery (refines OPS5 — costly, ~13 min).** The standalone `restart admin-portal` for the `quill` dep-refresh was **NOT isolated**: it triggered a workspace-wide Nx rebuild that momentarily emptied/regenerated the shared `dist/` (`@sanad/contracts`, `@sanad/database`, `shared/util`, `shared/messaging`), and the long-running backend containers (running `dist/main.js` under a restart wrapper) crash-looped on `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND` mid-rebuild; a plain `restart api-gateway` then hit the Nx **recursive-task-invocation** wedge from the poisoned per-service `*_nx` volume. **Fix (per OPS5):** for each affected backend service — `docker compose rm -sf <svc>` → `docker volume rm sanad-dev_<svc>_nx` → `up -d <svc>` (gateway/identity/content/media/notifications; settings self-recovered). After that all reads returned 200. **Lesson:** sequence a frontend dep-refresh so the shared `dist/` isn't rebuilt out from under live backend containers (pre-build shared libs, or quiet backend serve-watchers during the app-container install). See change-log 2026-06-28.

## Boot proof (2026-06-28, P7.C Stage 2 — portfolio)

Full stack up — **11/11 containers, restarts=0** after a one-time Nx-recursion recovery (below). Through nginx (`:8190`): `GET /api/health` → 200; `GET /admin/` → 200 `<html lang="ar" dir="rtl" data-app data-theme="dark">`. **New Vite modules transform (the stale-node_modules 500 check) → 200 `text/javascript`:** `…/modules/portfolio/components/portfolio-page.tsx` + `…/data/lookups/portfolio-category-lookup.api.ts`. **No dep-refresh needed** (zero new npm deps, as expected). Backend reads the vertical consumes (admin token): `GET /api/content/portfolio/manage` → 200 (`items:[]`, draft-visible admin list) + `GET /api/content/portfolio-categories` → 200 (5 seeded categories). The full portfolio + portfolio-categories CRUD route set is mapped at the gateway (pre-existing P5 routes — no new gateway routes).

**Nx-recursion recovery (refines `dev-stack-ops`).** On the cold `up`, **api-gateway** wedged with `NX Recursive task invocation detected (api-gateway:serve -> api-gateway:serve)`; identity + notifications transiently hit `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND` (stale shared-lib dist during concurrent build) and self-recovered on restart. **Key lesson:** host `npx nx reset` cleared identity/notifications but **did NOT** clear the gateway — its per-container `sanad-dev_gateway_nx` volume masks the bind-mounted `.nx`. Fix that worked: `stop` + `rm` the gateway container → `docker volume rm sanad-dev_gateway_nx` → `up -d api-gateway` alone → clean, `/api/health` 200. (The general rule already in memory — remove the `*_nx` volume, don't rely on `nx reset` — now has a gateway-specific confirmation.)

## Boot proof (2026-06-28, P7 descriptor-driven Settings)

Full stack up — **11/11 containers, restarts=0** after an **environmental Nx recovery** (below). Through nginx (`:8190`): admin login (`admin@sanad.tech`) → token; **`GET /api/settings` (admin) → 200** `SettingsCatalog` (`groups[0].group='branding'`; sections identity/colors/typography/social/contact; `branding.colors.primary` → `{type:'color', requirement:'optional', value:'#0ea5e9'}`); **no token → 401** (RBAC); **`PUT /api/settings {values:[{key:'branding.colors.primary',value:'#ff0000'}]}` → 200**; **public `GET /api/branding` → `colors.primary:'#ff0000'`** (proves the generic `SettingsWriter` backs branding + busts the Redis version cache); wrong-type value → rejected (500, see open follow-up); restored to `#0ea5e9` → 200. Admin: `GET /admin/` → 200 `<html lang="ar" dir="rtl">`; `…/modules/settings/components/settings-page.tsx` transforms through Vite → 200 `text/javascript`.

**Environmental Nx wedge + recovery (lesson).** The first boot-verify was DOWN: **settings-service then identity-service `serve` wedged with "NX Recursive task invocation detected (`serve -> serve`)"** — caused by running **host `nx`/verifier `nx run-many` commands while the dev stack was live** (the documented `dev-stack-ops` race). Each service's Nx state is a **per-service named volume** (`sanad-dev_<svc>_nx`), so **host `npx nx reset` does NOT clear it** (the recovery the memory listed was insufficient). The wedged services also left a **poison backlog** in `settings.commands` (24 msgs, 10 unacked — stale gateway requests whose reply queues had expired → handlers hung on reply). **Fix that worked:** `docker compose down` (no `-v`) → `docker volume rm sanad-dev_*_nx` (clears the recursion state; Postgres/Redis/RabbitMQ data preserved) → `docker compose up -d` (cold Nx rebuild) → purge stale command queues. After that every service booted clean and the flow above passed. **Takeaway:** don't run host `nx` against a live dev stack; to recover, remove the `*_nx` volumes (not just `nx reset`). (Memory `dev-stack-ops` updated.)

## Boot proof (2026-06-27, P7.C U21 remediation)

Full stack up — **11/11 containers up, restarts=0** (6 backend apps + admin-portal Vite + nginx + postgres/rabbitmq/redis; infra all healthy). Through nginx (`:8190`): `GET /api/health` → 200 (backend unaffected — 1106 routes mapped); `GET /admin/` → 200 `<html lang="ar" dir="rtl" data-app data-theme="dark">`. **The new dep + refactored modules transform through Vite (the exact 500 mode a stale node_modules volume hits) → all 200, real ES-module JS:** `…/components/ui/{select,textarea,badge,dialog}.tsx`, `…/components/dynamic-form/dynamic-form.tsx`, and the **`@radix-ui/react-select`** dist entrypoint (199 kB optimized chunk, v2.3.1, resolved from `apps/admin-portal/node_modules` → `.pnpm`). **Dep-refresh restart was required and recovered (expected — the documented one-time step for a new dep):** the admin-portal `admin_node_modules` volume was stale → Vite 500'd on `@radix-ui/react-select`; `docker compose … restart admin-portal` re-ran `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` → `Re-optimizing dependencies because lockfile has changed` → ready, zero errors after. Content backend the verticals consume still answers: `GET /api/content/services` → 200 well-formed paginated envelope. No crash-loop, no Nx recursive-task race.

Through nginx (`:8190`): `GET /api/health` → 200 (backend unaffected — this is a client-only change). **Admin-portal Vite (after the dep refresh):** `GET /admin/` → 200 `<html lang="ar" dir="rtl">`; `VITE … ready in 170 ms`; Vite **re-optimized** for the changed lockfile and transforms the new modules — `GET /admin/src/components/feedback/toast/{toast,toaster}.tsx` + `…/confirmation/confirmation-root.tsx` → **200** (was 500 before the refresh); `@radix-ui/react-toast` resolves from `apps/admin-portal/node_modules` → root `.pnpm` store. **Boot incident (recovered, environment-only — see open issue):** the first boot-verify was **DOWN** — the admin-portal container's `admin_node_modules` named volume was stale (it had been Up 4h, predating the new `@radix-ui/react-toast` dep), so Vite 500'd on the toast import while the SPA root mounts `<Toaster/>`. **Fix:** `docker compose … restart admin-portal` re-ran the container CMD's `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` against the updated lockfile → volume repopulated → Vite re-optimized → UP. No code change; verifier was already PASS.

## Boot proof (2026-06-24, P6)

Full stack up — **10/10 containers Up, restarts=0** (5 apps + nginx + postgres/rabbitmq/redis). The **5th app `notifications-service`** (host 3015→3005, DB `notifications`) booted with **two** `Nest microservice successfully started` lines (`notifications.commands` + `content.events`); RabbitMQ shows `LeadCreatedConsumer` bound to `content.events` (1 consumer). Gateway answers `GET /api/health` → 200 through nginx (`:8190`); P6 routes mapped (`GET/PUT /api/integration-secrets[/:key]`, `GET /api/notifications`, `PATCH /api/notifications/:id/read`). **Functional proof through nginx:** admin `PUT /api/integration-secrets/SMTP_PASSWORD {value}` → 200 masked status (`isSet:true,last4:"p-pw"`, no plaintext); `GET /api/integration-secrets` → `SMTP_PASSWORD isSet:true` + `R2_SECRET_KEY isSet:false`; `PUT` no token → 401. **Public** `POST /api/leads` (no token) → 201 `{id}`; notifications-service logs `[email:console] to=team@sanad.tech subject="New lead: Layla (saas)"` + `to=layla@example.com subject="استلمنا طلبك…"` (Arabic visitor ack via ConsoleEmailProvider — SMTP host unset in dev). Admin `GET /api/notifications` → 200 paginated, the `lead.created` in-app notice present; no token → 401. **Exactly-once:** the notifications DB holds exactly **3 rows + 1 `processed_message`** per lead; content's `lead.created` outbox rows all `published=t`. **Boot incident (recovered, no code change):** a single `up -d --build` recreating all 5 app containers on empty/uncached `.nx` volumes still triggered the Nx recursive-task-invocation crash (gateway) + the N11 dev-watch `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND` race (identity/content); remedy was per-container (clear the one app's `.nx` volume / restart one at a time) — never host `nx`. **Residual infra gotcha** (see open issues): even with per-container `.nx` volumes, a concurrent first-boot `--build` races; prefer staggered app-container startup or `up` without `--build` once images exist.
